- **subsidiarity-checker**: Use when running the mandatory subsidiarity and proportionality test on a draft Commission legislative proposal under Article 5(3)–(4) TEU and Protocol No. 2 on the application of the principles of subsidiarity and proportionality. Produces a structured subsidiarity and proportionality assessment suitable for inclusion in the Impact Assessment (SWD) or Explanatory Memorandum, compliant with the RSB (Regulatory Scrutiny Board) quality checklist and the Better Regulation Guidelines. Also produces the subsidiarity grid for Protocol No. 2 reasoned opinion risk assessment. ([montoyer/eu-agents](https://github.com/montoyer/eu-agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/eu-legislative/skills/subsidiarity-checker) · ★2)
- **subsidiarity-stress**: Tests the subsidiarity justification of a proposal against five different member-state configurations — varying by regulatory capacity, economic size, existing national legislation, and political orientation — to identify the configuration under which the subsidiarity check fails. A proposal that satisfies the subsidiarity test in Germany may fail it in Malta. This skill surfaces those failures before the text is written, so the proportionality and necessity arguments can be hardened in the impact assessment. Based on Protocol No. 2 (TEU/TFEU) and the Better Regulation subsidiarity test. ([montoyer/eu-agents](https://github.com/montoyer/eu-agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/eu-simulation/skills/subsidiarity-stress) · ★2)

Use whenever the user says '/subtraction', or asks to organise, structure, tidy, lay out, condense, or 'make sense of' a body of information, notes, research, options, status, findings or documentation. Also use proactively whenever an answer would otherwise be a long list, a dense table, many sections of prose, or a dump of everything you know when the reader needs only a s… ([Miyamura80/code-agent-config](https://github.com/Miyamura80/code-agent-config/tree/HEAD/claude-code/skills/subtraction))
- **subtraction-agent**: argue for removal. ([deagy/cadre](https://github.com/deagy/cadre/tree/HEAD/hermes-plugins/skills/cadre/review/subtraction-agent) · ★1)
- **subtraction-audit**: Use when evaluating whether code components earn their keep — especially when asking "is this over-engineered?", "what can we remove?", "this feels heavy", when inheriting unfamiliar code, before a major refactor, or after a feature is complete and feels bloated. ([jackwillis/claude-plugins](https://github.com/jackwillis/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/break-things/skills/subtraction-audit) · ★1)
- **subtraction-audit**: 代码减法审计与反腐化工程。用于给代码库做减法审计（找死代码/重复实现/死依赖/投机性泛化/死门禁），产出证据驱动的删除候选清单并按风险分级执行；也用于给 AI 重度开发的仓库搭建防膨胀门禁（CI、钩子、死代码基线）。核心信念：代码最终会腐化，人写机器写都一样；防腐化唯一可靠的方式是把约束变成机器门禁、把为什么变成文档、把删除变成一等工作流。方法学源自 DeepSeek Harness（dsh-find-simplifications）。 ([Howell5/willhong-skills](https://github.com/Howell5/willhong-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/subtraction-audit))
- **subtraction-design**: 設計判断の比較表で複数案を並列検討する場面、機能 X を追加する設計を選ぶ場面、アーキテクチャ選択を伴う設計時に「足す前に不要化できないか」を問う触媒として発火する ([VTRiot/ccpit-win](https://github.com/VTRiot/ccpit-win/tree/HEAD/golden/common/ja/skills/catalysts/subtraction-design) · ★9)
- **subtraction-design**: Fires when comparing multiple design options in parallel, when choosing a design that adds feature X, or when making an architecture choice — to ask "before adding, can it be made unnecessary?" as a catalyst. ([VTRiot/ccpit-win](https://github.com/VTRiot/ccpit-win/tree/HEAD/golden/common/en/skills/catalysts/subtraction-design) · ★9)
- **subtraction-first**: Use when improving existing code and the instinct is to add — a wrapper, a flag, a layer, a helper, a config option — before checking whether removing something is the better move; or when code feels bloated, over-engineered, or hard to hold in your head. Not for a full readability-and-elegance pass over working code — that's whittler ([atgreen/hackinator](https://github.com/atgreen/hackinator/tree/HEAD/skills/subtraction-first) · ★1)
- **subtraction-review**: 减法审:按周期清闸账、当日口径、预算表与历史附录,逐条判退役、续期还是转长期,产出减法审报告并登裁定簿。 ([comkocs/md-workflow](https://github.com/comkocs/md-workflow/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/subtraction-review) · ★4)
- **subtraction-thinking**: 對抗「只會加東西」的預設偏誤，在任何輸出前、途中、之後 MUST 強制執行減法審查。 減法有兩個方向：移除沒有存在理由的東西、簡化現有的東西讓它更直接有效。 適用於所有需要「做出某個東西」或「改善某個東西」的場合——商業決策、服務規劃、 組織設計、行銷方案、提案、流程、產品、報告、程式開發、skill 與規則檔皆適用。 當使用者要新增、改善、或問「還缺什麼」時 MUST 自動觸發，包含改 skill、改 CLAUDE.md 或 AGENTS.md、加規則、加工具。 ([TimLai666/skills](https://github.com/TimLai666/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/thinking-frameworks/skills/subtraction-thinking))
- **subtractive-codebase-consolidator**: Audit, plan, and verify local code reduction by responsibility, including duplicate authorities, parallel implementations, obsolete mechanisms, and their dependent residue. ([ablassejr/dotfiles](https://github.com/ablassejr/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/dot_claude/skills/subtractive-codebase-consolidator) · 2 copies)
- **subtractive-debugging**: Systematic regression isolation by subtracting from a known-good state. Invoke when a feature used to work, many changes landed, and you need the first cascaded mismatch instead of another hypothesis chain. Triggers on: regression, last-known-good, bisect, first-bad-change, shrink-the-surface. ([thistleknot/skills](https://github.com/thistleknot/skills/tree/HEAD/subtractive-debugging))
- **subtractive-design**: Simplify noisy interfaces and evaluate whether proposed UI additions serve a concrete user need. ([alencarljfrj/ui-skills](https://github.com/alencarljfrj/ui-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kemiljk--subtractive-design) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **subtractive-design**: Use when a landing plan contains repeated copy, decorative UI, competing actions, overlong sections, or redundant controls. Evaluate each element by the visitor task it supports; remove or combine only what can be removed without losing evidence, required content, context, access or an explicit user choice. ([Nick0oo/xpage](https://github.com/Nick0oo/xpage/tree/HEAD/agent/skills/subtractive-design))
- **subtractive-design-analyzer**: Analyze approved, repository-grounded work for safe replacement, consolidation, simplification, and removal opportunities before implementation decisions or code-mass budgeting. ([ablassejr/dotfiles](https://github.com/ablassejr/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/dot_agents/skills/subtractive-design-analyzer) · 3 copies)
- **subtractive-engineering**: Applies Occam's Razor ("do not multiply entities beyond necessity") to block additive engineering reflexes, prune dependency paths, and compress verbose text into high-density tables. ([gn00678465/agent-home](https://github.com/gn00678465/agent-home/tree/HEAD/.apm/skills/subtractive-engineering) · 2 copies)
- **subtractive-review**: Use when reviewing a diff, PR, plan, design, bug fix, process, or config change to counter subtractive neglect: the default bias to solve problems by adding code, layers, checks, steps, flags, or dependencies while overlooking the option to remove something. Forces a subtraction-first search, compares subtractive and additive options on net system complexity, and reports which one actually meets the goal. Not a general simplicity pass (use ponytail-review or a language simplifier for that). ([citadelgrad/scott-cc](https://github.com/citadelgrad/scott-cc/tree/HEAD/skills/subtractive-review) · ★3)
- **subtractive-review**: Review a codebase for safe deletions by pairing each removal with the invariant and evidence that make it safe. ([terebentina/skills](https://github.com/terebentina/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/subtractive-review))
- **subtrans-anime-ops**: Develop, test, and operate the Anime Subtitle Translation Engine (subtrans/, glossaries/). ([ThanhSi1008/ai-video-upscaler](https://github.com/ThanhSi1008/ai-video-upscaler/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/subtrans-anime-ops))
- **subtree-publish**: 将 monorepo 中的子目录通过 git subtree 推送到独立 GitHub 仓库。支持注册清单、变更自动检测、增量推送。本技能应在用户提交涉及已注册子项目的变更后，或手动请求推送到独立仓库时使用。不要用于初次创建 monorepo 或管理 git submodule。 ([cat-xierluo/legal-skills](https://github.com/cat-xierluo/legal-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/subtree-publish) · ★820)
- **subtree-upstream-governance**: Use when changing optional-mirror coordination, subtree sync policy, or maintainer-only subtree governance between consent-protocol and hushh-research. - **subtype-classifier-comparative-evaluation**: Use when a trained subtype classifier (e.g., DeepCC labels from get_DeepCC_label) has been applied to validation cohorts with clinical metadata and you must show its clinical relevance equals or exceeds a benchmark system like PAM50. - **subtype-concordance-evaluation**: Use when subtype calls produced by AWCA or mLR models on RNA-seq expression matrices (TCGA RSEM, GSE96058 FPKM, PanCA, GSE81538) must be scored against published PAM50 labels or against another caller's calls via concordance/accuracy. - **subtype-marker-identification**: Use when RCC (or another consensus clustering run) has produced cluster assignments on an expression matrix and you need per-cluster marker genes and signature-enrichment evidence to characterize novel subtypes. - **subtype-marker-selection**: Use when building subtype classification templates for pre-clinical CRC models (cell lines, organoids, PDXs) where microenvironment-derived gene signals must be excluded from the marker set. - **subtype-similarity-pattern-validation**: Use when reproducing a cross-dataset cell label similarity map (e.g., B cell subtypes across HCC, NSCLC, BRCA references) and checking whether recomputed Jaccard values reproduce a reported pattern from the scCancer2 paper. - **subtyping-comparison-visualization**: Use when you need to cross-compare intrinsic breast cancer subtype callers (Prosigna, standard PAM50, AWCA-based PAM50) on the same RNA-seq profiles and visualize call concordance plus ROR prognostic agreement.
